16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">延长寿命或逆转衰老就是减少稀缺性，它将充裕的时间归还给每个人。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">超人类主义是指人类利用技术实现自我提升。这是一个非常广泛的领域。超人类主义包括自我监测、外部设备（如手机、手表、眼镜、耳塞、隐形眼镜）、身体改造、超级士兵血清（查看肌生成抑制蛋白的缺失情况）、脑机接口（如Neuralink公司）</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">即使我们只能实现其中的一部分，并将其应用到数百万人身上，也将会产生巨大影响。其影响将是发明眼镜所带来的影响的1000倍。然而，超人类主义遭到了反对者的抵制，他们担心人类发生任何变化，无论这种变化是好是坏。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">在整个进化时间尺度上，我们已经与技术共同进化了很长一段时间。技术实际上造就了人类，并使我们与动物相区别。简单来说，如何先让个人升级，然后让群体升级，最后让整个人类升级，就是我对世界的看法。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">真相</span></span></span></p><p><span leaf=""><br  /></span><span leaf=""><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);font-weight: bold;">*</span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);font-weight: bold;">真相大多不流行，流行大多非真相。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">区分社会共识和真相非常重要，因为它们是不一样的。我们常常会基于社会共识来判断某事是否为真。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">你可以从两个极端的角度来看待事实：政治事实和技术事实。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">如果有足够多的人相信一个政治事实是真实的，那么它就是真实的，例如总统是谁或国家边界在哪里。这其实是一种心理现象。如果你能在足够多人的大脑中安装软件，你就能改变这些社会共识。而另外一些事情是纯粹的技术事实。技术事实是方程的解或电子显微镜下病毒的直径，它是物理常量的结果。人们的想法不会改变技术真相。物理事实独立于任何人类个体，就算是外星人也会得出同样的结论。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">确定人们接受的证据类型与了解他们的动机一样重要。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">有些人接受数据，但很多人只接受流行度。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">如果一件事不能够被独立复现，那么它就不是科学。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">当中央集权机构变得过于僵化时，去中心化将成为文明的开伞索。马丁·路德用它来论证“个人与上帝的关系”，使人们不再需要作为中间人的教廷。乔治·华盛顿、中本聪也曾使用过它。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">自然现象是去中心化的。任何人都应该能够检验你的算法或有关电场的陈述是否正确。技术史是有效之术的历史，政治史是固权之术的历史。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">政治从根本上讲是关于群体的，而不是关于真相的。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">如果每个人都相信政治真相是真实的，比如金钱、地位和边境线，那么它们就是真实的。你可以通过改写人们大脑中的事实来改变它们，例如，一美元的价值是多少？总统是谁？边境线在哪里？我们的体制就是为了操纵这些真相而建立的。在政治中，几乎没有人有动机去说出可能惹恼你所属群体的真相。导致你的群体处于劣势的真相是一个不会被公之于众的真相。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">记者迈克尔·金斯利说：“当一位政治家说出真相，说出那些他本不应说出的明显真相时，他就失言了。”</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">许多人不会基于逻辑前提进行正向推理，而擅长从社会后果出发进行反推。这种行为有它自己的逻辑，因为社会资本本质上还是资本。对外部群体大喊大叫比对内部群体进行投资要便宜得多。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">现代媒体与真相背道而驰</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">媒体就像一面闪闪发光的镜子。现实在远处，你读到的内容在近处，而媒体控制着中间的映射过程。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">“争议性论断”(scissor statement)是指对一方来说明显正确，而对另一方来说明显错误的陈述。媒体和社交媒体公司不断寻找和挑选这种争议性论断，因为它们能触犯众怒，从而吸引流量。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">有一类公司，记者会永远捍卫它，那就是媒体公司。有一种平等形式，记者会永远抵制，那就是人人皆记者。直接的竞争对手不可能成为公正的仲裁者。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">马斯克、扎克伯格和所有最聪明的创始人都在建立自己的媒体部门，绕过传统媒体公司，直接发布信息。这已成为一项核心竞争力。不去设立直接发布渠道的首席执行官或创始人，就没走上正确的道路。这就像一家公司不设立官方网站一样。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">重新定位媒体</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">也许新时代的本地新闻是按知识垂类划分的，而不是按地理区域划分的。这并不是要从大量用户身上赚钱，而是要为真正需要理解和领会某个想法的人服务。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">教程不能对你“撒谎”，这也不是它们的本意。它们不能歪曲任何事情，不能低估复杂性或夸大结果。正因为它们是指导性的，所以你在使用它们的同时，也在不断学习并进行事实核查。这不是一件显而易见的事，人们通常不会去思考这一过程。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">天气或股票价格是另一类可用新闻。这类信息直接决定人们的实际行动。如果下雨，我就带把伞。如果股价高，也许我会卖出。如果股价低，也许我会买入。在我们理想中的媒体分类图谱上，这类指导决策的信息紧挨着教程这一类别。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">你甚至需要穷尽一生才能记住地球上70亿人的名字。因此，你不可能平等地关心所有人。你必须对信息进行分类，或对你所关注的事情进行优先级排序。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">什么是真正的好东西？它可以帮助你优化你所关心的可测量变量，例如你掌握的真相，拥有的健康和财富。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">它涉及你的知识、身体健康、银行账户余额，或者它们的某种组合。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">你每天看到的第一件事不应该是别人挑选的随机故事，而应该是你精挑细选的想要改善的指标，例如你的健康或爱好。打造个人仪表盘是颠覆报纸行业的好方法。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">你要做的是寻找下一个像核聚变能源领域的扎克伯格或维塔利克这样的人，把他们吸引到你的博客，并帮助他们建功立业。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">在全球范围内，你可能只有10000名读者，他们是世上为数不多的足够关心核聚变能源并将你的文章视为“可用新闻”的人。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">比特币教会了我们这一点。在对一项技术的宣传和推广中，如果支持技术的一方在用写作阐述其价值和意义时，展现出更充沛的能量和更强烈的热忱，那么从长远来看，这项技术将会取得成功。我们不仅仅需要写作，还需要艺术、文学和电影来推广技术。不是必须成为火箭科学家，才能发表支持他们的言论。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">打造更好的真相机器</span></span></span></p><p sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">现在，是时候研究精神上的传染病了。我们都把心思花在了推特上。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">使用推特的过程就像把你的大脑放入一个装有其他3亿人大脑的大缸中，人们将他们的大脑状态以电磁信号的方式发送给你。这么一想就感觉有点儿不卫生了。我们在线下保持社交距离，在线上却挤成一团。低劣的梗图和疯狂的想法比以往任何时候都传播得更快，因为我们所有人的大脑都相互连接在一起。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br 16px;">你实际上是在做什么呢？无需自然资源，只需敲击按键，你就造出了一套令人愉悦的电子配置。从根本上来说，你从无序中创造了秩序，把随机排列的0和1变成了更有用的组合配置。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">财富的创造就是技术相关的秩序创造。这就像一堆砖头和一座房子、一堆木头和一把椅子的区别。你可以在物理空间中看到这些差异。当人们对现实生活中的财富创造感到困惑时，他们会说：“你一定是从别人那里得到了砖块和木头。”然而，</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">大部分价值存在于这些材料秩序化的过程中。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">我花钱是为了让自己能更努力地工作，这听起来很滑稽，但却是事实。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">我不是一个消费型的人，而是一个产出型的人。我不会在一件事情上烧钱。一切都将进入下一个复利循环。这不仅意味着金钱的复利，尽管金钱是一个重要的工具。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(178, 178, 178);font-style: italic;">（注：找到你一辈子想做的事，开始行动，在过程中持续创造财富，在入大于出的那一刻，你就已经财富自由了。财务自由从来不是指你有多少钱，而是指你赚钱的方法是否足够独立自由。）</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">然后，你可以从一个在旧金山工作、年薪12万美元、年支出10万美元、没有积蓄的人，转变为在印度尼西亚的巴厘岛工作、年薪12万美元、每年只花3万或4万美元的人，后者的生活质量更高。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">如果你经济独立，大众就无法在经济上控制你。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">从民族主义者转变为资本主义者、技术主义者，难度会变得越来越大，但从中长期来看，这会为社会增加更多的价值。所有其他群体都受益于技术主义者。飞机能飞了，火车也能跑了。我们现在认为这一切都是理所当然的，但恰恰是因为一小群人的努力，技术才得以发展壮大。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">想要影响科技的发展方向，要么拿起键盘开始行动，要么投入资本承担风险。你可以建造一些东西，而那些不会建造的人只会四处空谈。全球数十亿人中，越来越多的人开始拿起键盘，他们明白空谈和实际去创造东西相比，哪个更有价值，哪个效用更大。真正愤世嫉俗的人和真正温顺的人有一个共同点：他们从不采取大胆的行动。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">创立</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);font-weight: bold;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);font-weight: bold;">创始人通常是那个唯一有信誉、可以为了更大的长期收益而付出巨大的短期成本的人。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">美国东海岸非常注重传承。这显然与财富继承有关，</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">但也关涉家族姓氏的传承，例如肯尼迪和布什。人们继承整个机构，就像默多克家族、格雷厄姆家族和苏兹伯格家族继承报纸一样。这与美国西海岸模式有很大不同，</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">西海岸模式是关于创立的。SpaceX、亚马逊、脸书等都不是继承来的。它们都是从零开始创建的。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">马克·扎克伯格为何担任脸书的首席执行官？因为他创立了脸书。他20岁就担任了首席执行官，靠的不是说服30亿人的一张嘴。他获得的每一份支持——一名员工、一位用户、一个广告客户、一个反向链接——都是随时间的推移逐渐积累的。这涉及一系列一对一的交易，其间他给予每个人的利益都比其之前得到的更多，这促成了双赢交易。这些资源不断涌入，整个过程很快，资源呈指数级增长，但它并不是在一天之内暴增的。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">这就是创立和继承之间的区别，也是同时选择合法性和能力与仅选择合法性之间的区别。国家比任何一个人都拥有更多的钱。NASA（美国国家航空航天局）支持SpaceX的原因是技术不再受资本限制，而受能力限制。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">只基于合法性的选择通常意味着一个机构的现任领导人没有从头开始创立它的能力。纽约第75任市长和美国第50任总统并不是那种能够从无到有建立新制度的人。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">选拔机制确实非常重要，因为领导者需要理解的不仅仅是系统的当前状态，还有实现该状态的路径。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">失眠一晚并不意味着世界末日到了，失眠一年则会影响你的长期健康。即使从纯粹的金钱角度来看，失眠过多也会影响你的财富创造能力，以及为员工和股东提供价值的能力。虽然牺牲睡眠在日常生活中看上去是一种奉献之举，但从长远来看，为了你周围人的健康，你也要照顾好自己的健康。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">当你起床时，应为自己留出一些专注的时间。这样的话，你每天至少有几个小时可以按照你自己决定的方向前进。假设你7点或8点起床，锻炼到9点，保持离线状态到下午1点，这时你就已经连续深度工作4个小时了。世界上没有人可以打扰你，没有人可以联系你，没有人可以在推特上找你。你对于整个世界来说都处于离线状态。这是个很好的方法，因为你能够集中推进你的优先事项。然后你上线，与世界同步，展示你所有的进展。现在你处于主动出击的状态。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">在推特以外取胜才能在推特上取胜。几乎任何你想要的东西都无法靠推特获得，你必须在推特之外获得成功，然后在推特上广而告之。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">“努力工作是一种竞争优势”这一信念本身，如今也是一种竞争优势。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">最新的技术论文和最古老的书籍是最好的套利来源，它们包含最冷门的事实和最能赚钱的真相。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">你所了解的那些别人不能或不愿意承认的真相，就是你的竞争优势。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">布莱恩·切斯基从19世纪末撰写的一系列有关合租房的文章中得到启发，创建了爱彼迎。事实上，合租房在1900年左右比在1950年要普遍得多。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">不是每个人都是图灵，就像不是每个人都是托尔斯泰一样，但普遍的编程能力就像普遍的读写能力一样，应该被普及。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">高峰和低谷只有在回看时才会显而易见。在行动的那一刻，只有你逆势而行。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><br  /></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);font-weight: bold;">*</span></span></span><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, bold;">我曾放弃了很多很有前景的金融投资，因为我对只赚钱的项目不感兴趣。我的投资是基于意识形态的投资，我投资的是我希望看到被建设出来的世界。</span></span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, 0px;"><span><span leaf="" style="color:rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size:17px;mp-quote&quot;, -apple-system-font, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;PingFang SC&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:0.034em;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">技术最棒的特点是什么？那就是“过去的事就让它过去”。总有新一列火车离开车站，新一枚火箭升空。找到它、资助它或加入它。我们必须努力创造我们想要的未来。</span></span></span></p><p style="text-align: center;margin-bottom: 0px;"><span style="font-size: 15px;"><span
